##### Tools

* [ai-evaluation (⭐128)](https://github.com/future-agi/agent-learning-kit) ⭐ 128 | 🐛 30 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-31 - Open-source LLM and agent evaluation framework with 50+ metrics, LLM-as-Judge augmentation, and guardrail scanners (jailbreak, PII, prompt-injection). Useful for scoring RAG outputs, agent trajectories, and function-calling behavior in data-science workflows.

#### [3. Awesome Iam](/content/kdeldycke/awesome-iam/README.md)

##### Trust & Safety / Fraud

* [United States v. Javice](https://storage.courtlistener.com/recap/gov.uscourts.nysd.596549/gov.uscourts.nysd.596549.1.0_1.pdf) - Counting records is not verifying identities. A startup with 293,192 users claimed 4.265 million to its buyer, and had a data scientist generate a synthetic file of that size. The validation did not catch it: “Neither Frank nor JPMC asked Vendor-1 to verify the authenticity or accuracy of data in the Synthetic Data Set, but instead retained Vendor-1 to count the number of records and verify the percentage of the submitted data fields that were populated with data.”

Describe a UI in plain words and get web, Figma and SwiftUI output checked by deterministic taste gates; installs with `npx @easestart/design-os init`. Source-available (PolyForm Shield).
* [Ironheights (⭐2)](https://github.com/Frank-Masciopinto/ironheights) ⭐ 3 | 🐛 4 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-11-01 - Offline security scanner that protects any AI bot's skills: scans any skill folder with a `SKILL.md` (including Gemini CLI skills in `~/.gemini/skills/`) for prompt injection, credential theft, and other risky patterns before you install it, with findings tagged by OWASP LLM Top 10 and OpenCRE ID. Reads files as data, never runs them, no telemetry. Run `npx ironheights scan ./path/to/skill`. Apache-2.0.

##### MCP Servers

* [GetYouTubeTranscript (⭐23)](https://github.com/tubeagentkit/youtube-mcp) ⭐ 24 | 🐛 2 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-10-30 - YouTube transcripts and data for Gemini CLI: fetch a video's transcript (pick the language, optional timestamps), search YouTube, browse a channel's videos, playlists and posts, search inside a channel, list a playlist, find related videos, and read a channel banner or estimated dislikes. 13 tools. Hosted Streamable HTTP; tool calls need an API key from the [dashboard](https://getyoutubetranscript.com/dashboard?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) (1 credit per call, 100 credits on sign-up, paid plans from $5/month). MIT. Add with: `gemini mcp add --transport http --header "Authorization: Bearer <API_KEY>" getyoutubetranscript https://getyoutubetranscript.com/api/mcp`.
* [recall (⭐2)](https://github.com/fainir/recall) ⭐ 2 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-11-01 - MCP server that reads and writes Claude Code's per-project auto memory (Markdown files with `[[wiki-links]]`), so Gemini CLI, Codex and Cursor share the same memories. `memory_recall` follows links within a token budget; optional read-only mode. Zero dependencies, local only. MIT. Install with `gemini extensions install https://github.com/fainir/recall` (serves the memory of the folder you start Gemini in) or run `npx -y @fainir/recall`.

#### [9. Awesome Amazon Seller](/content/ScaleLeap/awesome-amazon-seller/README.md)

##### Software and Tools

* [BulkBarcode Generator](https://bulkbarcode-generator.com) - Makes FNSKU, UPC, EAN-13, Code 128 and QR labels in bulk from an Excel or CSV file, and exports print-ready PDF rolls for thermal printers. Runs in the browser. Free plan.
* [CorpusIQ](https://www.corpusiq.io) - Connects ChatGPT, Claude and Perplexity read-only to Amazon Seller, Shopify, GA4, QuickBooks and 40+ other systems, and answers questions about the business with the source of each number. From $29.95 a month, with a 30-day free trial.
* [Fbaleaks](https://fbaleaks.com) - Audits FBA inventory and reimbursement reports in the browser. Finds lost or damaged units that Amazon did not reimburse, sorts SKUs to keep, reprice or remove, and drafts appeal letters. Free tools and a one-time paid audit.
* [PixVibe](https://pixvibe.ai/tools/remove-background) - Browser-based background remover that exports product photos on a white or transparent background, with tools to compress, resize and crop images. Free for single images, with no account.
* [TranslateMyImage](https://translatemyimage.com/amazon-image-translator) - Translates the text inside product images, infographics, A+ modules and size charts into other languages, and keeps the original layout. Works from the final JPG or PNG files. Three free test images.

##### Product Research and Pre-Launch

* [Trends MCP](https://www.trendsmcp.ai) - MCP server and REST API with trend data from Amazon, Google Search, Google Shopping, TikTok, YouTube and other sources, for demand research in an AI assistant. Free plan with 100 requests a month. [GitHub (⭐44)](https://github.com/trendsmcp-ai/Trends-MCP) ⭐ 44 | 🐛 5 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-08-29

##### Other

* [Awesome Agentic Commerce (⭐65)](https://github.com/MentionNetwork/awesome-agentic-commerce) ⭐ 65 | 🐛 146 | 📅 2026-07-20 - Curated list of agentic commerce resources: protocols (ACP, UCP, MCP), agent-readiness tools, and guides to make products visible to AI shopping agents.
